### [1] [MotionMERGE: A Multi-granular Framework for Human Motion Editing, Reasoning, Generation, and Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18956)
*Bizhu Wu,Jinheng Xie,Wenting Chen,Zhe Kong,Jianfeng Ren,Linlin Shen,Ruibin Bai,Rong Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionMERGE是一个统一框架，通过细粒度部分和时间级建模、交叉粒度对齐预训练以及大规模精调数据集MotionFineEdit，弥合了运动-语言模型在理解和生成中的粒度鸿沟，实现了更精确的运动生成、理解、编辑和类人推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动-语言模型虽然统一了理解与生成任务，但操作粒度粗糙，缺乏对细粒度身体部位的理解和精确控制，无法满足动画或交互需求。根本问题在于模型无法关注运动的局部模式，且训练数据缺乏细粒度监督。

Method: 1) 在单个LLM中显式建模部分级和时间级运动，实现细粒度语言引导的运动控制；2) 提出Reasoning-Aware Granularity-Synergy预训练策略，联合监督跨粒度对齐、时间定位、局部对齐、运动连贯性和运动接地思维链推理；3) 构建MotionFineEdit大规模数据集（837K原子+144K复杂三元组），包含细粒度时空校正指令和运动接地CoT标注。

Result: 广泛实验证明MotionMERGE在运动生成、理解和编辑上更精确，并在其他复杂运动任务上展现出令人信服的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作向更细粒度、类人推理的运动交互模型迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Recent motion-language models unify tasks like comprehension and generation but operate at a coarse granularity, lacking fine-grained understanding and nuanced control over body parts needed for animation or interaction. This stems from fundamental issues in both the model and the data, in which the model can't focus on motion's localized pattern, and the training data lacks fine-grained supervision. To tackle this, we propose MotionMERGE, a unified framework that bridges the granularity gap. First, we pioneer the study of fine-grained languageguided motion control, including detailed understanding and localized editing, by explicitly modeling motion at part and temporal levels within a single LLM, thereby endowing the model with robust priors for precise control. Second, we design ReasoningAware Granularity-Synergy pre-training, a novel strategy that employs joint supervision for cross-granularity alignment, temporal grounding, localized alignment, motion coherency, and motion-grounded chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning. This equips the model with fine-grained motion-language alignment, crossgranularity synergy, and explicit reasoning ability. Third, we curate MotionFineEdit, a large-scale dataset (837K atomic + 144K complex triplets) with the first fine-grained spatio-temporal corrective instructions and motion-grounded CoT annotations, establishing a new benchmark for fine-grained text-driven motion editing and motion-grounded reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate the capability of MotionMERGE for more precise motion generation, understanding, and editing, and compelling zero-shot generalization to other complex motion tasks. This work represents a significant step toward models that interact with motion in finer granularity and human-like reasoning.

</details>


### [2] [Harnessing Self-Supervised Features for Art Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18974)
*Federico Melis,Davide Bilardello,Emanuele Prato,Evelyn Turri,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了监督学习和自监督学习主干网络在艺术品分类和检索任务中的表现，发现自监督学习主干网络（尤其是DINO系列和CLIP模型）能持续提升分类性能，并探索了其在VR博物馆导航等实际应用中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 艺术品分类因细粒度细节与抽象特征的交织而具有挑战性，现有方法在区分不同风格或流派的作品上效果有限，需要更有效的特征提取方法。

Method: 系统评估了DINO系列和CLIP等自监督模型与监督学习模型作为特征提取器的效果，测试了多种分类策略和特征表示方法，并在绘画数据集上进行实验。

Result: 自监督学习主干网络在艺术品分类任务上持续优于监督学习方法。

Conclusion: 自监督特征提取器在艺术品理解和检索任务中具有显著优势，可用于虚拟现实博物馆导航等实际应用场景。

Abstract: Classifying artworks presents a significant challenge due to the complex interplay of fine-grained details and abstract features that condition the style or genre of an artwork. This paper presents a systematic investigation of the effectiveness of supervised and self-supervised backbones as feature extractors for both artwork classification and retrieval, with a particular focus on paintings. We conduct an extensive experimental evaluation using the DINO family and CLIP models, assessing multiple classification strategies and feature representations. Our results demonstrate that employing a self-supervised backbone leads to consistent improvements in artwork classification performance. Moreover, our work provides insights into the applicability of classification and retrieval modules in real-world applications, such as virtual reality (VR) applications that support museum navigation.

</details>


### [3] [Artifact-Bench: Evaluating MLLMs on Detecting and Assessing the Artifacts of AI-Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18984)
*Yuqi Tang,Yang Shi,Zhuoran Zhang,Qixun Wang,Xuehai Bai,Yue Ding,Ruizhe Chen,Bohan Zeng,Xinlong Chen,Xuanyu Zhu,Bozhou Li,Yuran Wang,Yifan Dai,Chengzhuo Tong,Xinyu Liu,Yiyan Ji,Yujie Wei,Yuhao Dong,Shilin Yan,Fengxiang Wang,Yi-Fan Zhang,Haotian Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Artifact-Bench基准，用于系统评估多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)在AI生成视频中检测和推理各类伪影（如时间不一致、结构扭曲、语义不连贯）的能力。实验发现19个主流MLLMs在伪影感知和推理上存在显著局限，且与人类感知偏好存在严重错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成视频虽逼真度提升，但仍存在各类伪影；而MLLMs对这类伪影的感知和推理能力尚不明确，且现有基准缺乏对伪影感知的系统评估和细粒度诊断推理。

Method: 构建了三级层次化的真实感伪影分类体系（涵盖照片级、动画、CG风格视频），定义了三项互补任务：真实vs AI生成视频分类、成对真实感比较、细粒度伪影识别。在19个主流MLLMs上进行实验评估。

Result: 多数MLLMs在伪影感知和推理上表现接近随机甚至低于随机水平，且模型判断与人类感知偏好之间存在显著错位。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs作为AI生成视频真实感的通用评估器可靠性有限，亟需改进其伪影感知与推理能力。

Abstract: Recent video generative models have greatly improved the realism of AI-generated videos, yet their outputs still exhibit artifacts such as temporal inconsistencies, structural distortions, and semantic incoherence. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show strong visual understanding capabilities, their ability to perceive and reason about such artifacts remains unclear. Existing benchmarks often lack systematic evaluation of artifact-aware perception and fine-grained diagnostic reasoning, especially across diverse AI-generated video domains beyond photorealistic content. To address this gap, we introduce Artifact-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on AI-generated video artifact detection and analysis. We first establish a three-level hierarchical taxonomy of realism artifacts, covering photorealistic, animated, and CG-style videos. Based on this taxonomy, Artifact-Bench defines three complementary tasks: real vs. AI-generated video classification, pairwise realism comparison, and fine-grained artifact identification. Experiments on 19 leading MLLMs reveal substantial limitations in artifact perception and reasoning, with many models approaching random or even below-random performance in challenging settings. We further observe significant misalignment between MLLM judgments and human perceptual preferences, highlighting their limited reliability as general evaluators for AI-generated video realism.

</details>


### [4] [EgoTraj: Real-World Egocentric Human Trajectory Dataset for Multimodal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19004)
*Ahmad Yehia,Abduallah Mohamed,Tianyi Wang,Jiseop Byeon,Kun Qian,Junfeng Jiao,Christian Claudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoTraj是一个基于Meta Quest Pro收集的自我中心视角多模态轨迹数据集，包含75段在城市环境中的人类导航序列，同步记录了RGB视频、6自由度头部姿态、3D眼动视线和场景标注，并验证了其在轨迹预测任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视角的人类轨迹预测在类人机器人、可穿戴传感和辅助导航等应用中至关重要，但缺乏真实世界中的自我中心轨迹数据集限制了该领域的发展。

Method: 使用Meta Quest Pro眼镜在真实城市环境中收集了75段导航序列，同步记录RGB视频、6自由度头部姿态、3D眼动视线和场景标注，构建为多模态开放数据集EgoTraj。

Result: 在EgoTraj上对多种现有自我中心轨迹预测方法进行了基准测试，并通过消融实验分析了视线、场景和运动线索的贡献，验证了数据集的有效性。

Conclusion: EgoTraj数据集为增强现实感知、导航和辅助系统提供了有价值的资源，数据集、代码及可视化工具均已公开。

Abstract: Accurately forecasting human trajectories from an egocentric perspective plays a central role in applications such as humanoid robotics, wearable sensing systems, and assistive navigation. However, progress in this direction remains limited due to the scarcity of egocentric trajectory datasets collected in real-world environments. Addressing this need, we introduce EgoTraj, an egocentric multimodal open dataset recorded using Meta Quest Pro (MQPro). EgoTraj contains 75 sequences of human navigation collected from multiple MQPro wearers in real-world urban environments. Each recording provides synchronized RGB video along with ground-truth data, including continuous time-synchronized 6-degree-of-freedom head poses, per-frame 3D eye gaze vectors, scene annotations. To the best of our knowledge, EgoTraj differs from typical egocentric trajectory datasets by capturing long-horizon, self-directed navigation across diverse urban routes with broad participant diversity. To demonstrate the potential of the dataset, we benchmark several state-of-the-art methods for egocentric trajectory prediction and conduct ablation studies to analyze the contributions of gaze, scene, and motion cues. The results highlight the utility of EgoTraj for AR-based perception, navigation, and assistive systems. The EgoTraj dataset, code, and EgoViz Dashboard are publicly available at https://github.com/yehiahmad/EgoTraj.

</details>


### [5] [A Systematic Failure Analysis of Vision Foundation Models for Open Set Iris Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19020)
*Rahul Anand,Siddharth Singh,Dileep A D,Mahadeva Prasanna,Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统性分析了通用的视觉基础模型在虹膜呈现攻击检测（PAD）中的失败情况，发现模型无法泛化到未见攻击工具，在跨光谱场景下性能严重下降，表明闭集或跨数据集上的优秀表现不能证明开放集的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然视觉基础模型在多种视觉识别任务中展示了强大的迁移能力并开始被考虑用于生物识别，但其在虹膜呈现攻击检测（PAD）中的适用性，尤其是在现实的开放集操作条件下，尚未得到充分检验。

Method: 评估了5个代表性基础模型，在3种开放集协议下测试（未见攻击工具PAI、不同传感器的跨数据集、近红外到可见光的跨光谱迁移），同时评估了冻结特征表示和LoRA参数高效微调两种方式。

Result: 基础模型在相似传感特征的数据集间能迁移，但对未见攻击工具泛化不可靠，跨光谱评估下性能急剧下降。LoRA在跨数据集设置下有所提升，但在攻击层面和光谱偏移下常放大失败。

Conclusion: 强闭集或跨数据集性能不应被视为鲁棒开放集安全的证据，PAD表示需要在保持对呈现伪影敏感性的同时，在现实部署变化下保持稳定。

Abstract: Vision foundation models have demonstrated strong transferability across diverse visual recognition tasks and are increasingly considered for biometric applications. Their suitability for iris Presentation Attack Detection (PAD), particularly under realistic open-set operating conditions, remains insufficiently examined. This work presents a systematic failure analysis of general-purpose vision foundation models for open-set iris PAD using periocular imagery. Five representative foundation models are evaluated under three open-set protocols that explicitly separate different sources of distribution shift: unseen Presentation Attack Instruments (PAIs), unseen datasets captured with different sensors and cross-spectral transfer from near-infrared (NIR) to visible spectrum (VIS) imagery. Both frozen feature representations and parameter-efficient task adaptation using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) are assessed within a unified experimental framework. The results indicate that foundation models can transfer across datasets with similar sensing characteristics, but fail to generalise reliably to unseen attack instruments and degrade sharply under cross-spectral evaluation. While LoRA improves performance in certain cross-dataset settings, it frequently amplifies failure under attack-level and spectral shifts. Additional validation experiments using segmented iris inputs, full backbone fine-tuning, joint cross-dataset and cross-PAI shifts, and reverse VIS to NIR transfer further confirm that these failures are not simply artefacts of periocular input, weak adaptation, or one-directional spectral evaluation. These findings show that strong closed-set or cross-dataset performance should not be treated as evidence of robust open-set security, and highlight the need for PAD representations that maintain sensitivity to presentation artefacts while remaining stable under realistic deployment variation.

</details>


### [6] [MedFM-Robust: Benchmarking Robustness of Medical Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19027)
*Xiangxiang Cui,Tianjin Huang,Yifang Wang,Lijie Hu,Lu Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了医疗基础模型（MedFMs）的两大范式：医学视觉语言模型（如LLaVA-Med、GPT-4o）和分割基础模型（如SAM衍生模型），并强调了临床部署前进行可靠性评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗基础模型在临床应用中展现出巨大潜力，但缺乏对其在实际条件下可靠性的系统评估，因此需要对这两类模型进行全面的可靠性分析。

Method: 将MedFMs分为两类：医学视觉语言模型（专用模型如LLaVA-Med、MedGemma，通用模型如GPT-4o、Gemini）和分割基础模型（SAM及其医学变体如SAM-Med2D、MedSAM）。

Result: 医疗基础模型在VQA、报告生成、视觉定位和医学图像分割等任务上展现出能力，但临床部署前的可靠性评估仍面临挑战。

Conclusion: 随着MedFMs在临床的广泛应用，必须对其在真实条件下的可靠性进行严格评估，以确保安全有效的临床部署。

Abstract: Medical foundation models (MedFMs) have emerged as transformative tools in healthcare, demonstrating capabilities across diverse clinical applications. These models can be broadly categorized into two paradigms: Medical Vision-Language Models (Med-VLMs) and segmentation foundation models. Med-VLMs range from medical-specialized models such as LLaVA-Med and MedGemma, to general-purpose models like GPT-4o and Gemini, all capable of medical image understanding tasks including visual question answering (VQA), report generation, and visual grounding. Concurrently, the Segment Anything Model (SAM) has catalyzed a new generation of medical segmentation models, with adaptations like SAM-Med2D and MedSAM. The widespread clinical deployment of these models thus necessitates rigorous evaluation of their reliability under real-world conditions.

</details>


### [7] [Personalized Face Privacy Protection From a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19032)
*Zachary Yahn,Fatih Ilhan,Tiansheng Huang,Selim Tekin,Sihao Hu,Yichang Xu,Margaret Loper,Ling Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaceCloak 是一个基于单张照片生成个性化面部隐私保护遮罩的系统，通过三阶段学习扰动，使面部识别失效，在十个识别模型上优于29种现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上传到网上的面部照片容易被恶意爬取并用于未经授权的面部识别，侵犯个人隐私。现有方法存在不足，需要更有效、轻量且保持视觉质量的个性化面部隐私保护方案。

Method: 三阶段学习：(1)基于单张图像生成高多样性合成人脸图像；(2)通过迭代扰动生成，将用户身份嵌入向远处锚点移位并远离相似身份，重点保护关键面部识别区域；(3)生成像素级个性化防护遮罩，轻量高效可应用于任何用户面部图像。

Result: 在三个流行面部数据集、十个识别模型上的实验表明，FaceCloak 相比29种现有代表方法效果更优。

Conclusion: FaceCloak 实现了单图驱动的个性化面部隐私保护，具备高有效性、轻量级和良好感知质量等综合优势。

Abstract: Photos of faces uploaded online are vulnerable to malicious actors who can scrape facial images from online sources and intrude on personal privacy via unauthorized use of facial recognition models. This paper presents FaceCloak, a novel personalized face privacy protection system, which can generate defensive identity-specific universal face privacy masks from a single image of a user, causing facial recognition to fail. FaceCloak introduces a three-stage personalized face perturbation learning methodology: (1) It generates a small set of high-variety synthetic face images of a person based on a single image of the person. (2) It learns face cloaking by adding more protection to key facial-identity leakage regions through iterative perturbation generation over the small set of synthetic images, effectively shifting a user's identity embedding towards a distant anchor identity and away from a similar one. (3) It generates a personalized identity-protective mask in the form of pixel-wise cloaking, which is light-weight and can be efficiently applied to any facial image of a user while maintaining good perceptual quality. Extensive experiments on three popular face datasets across ten recognition models show the effectiveness of FaceCloak compared to 29 other existing representative methods. Code is available at https://github.com/zacharyyahn/FaceCloak

</details>


### [8] [LiFT: Lifted Inter-slice Feature Trajectories for 3D Image Generation from 2D Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19060)
*Xinhe Zhang,Yuyang Zhang,Pengfei Jin,Arnau Marin-Llobet,Na Li,Quanzheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiFT框架，通过分解3D体素生成为逐切片图像生成和切片间轨迹学习，在降低计算成本的同时保持解剖一致性，在BraTS 2023和SynthRAD2023上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全3D体素模型计算成本高，而2D切片生成器无法保持第三维的解剖一致性，需要一种兼顾效率与三维一致性的方法。

Method: 将3D体素视为特征空间的有序轨迹，用逐切片生成+切片间轨迹学习分解；提出三平面漂移损失对齐生成轨迹与真实轨迹；双向z-上下文混合器用于配对翻译。

Result: 在无条件生成中保持切片质量；缺失模态MR重建接近cWDM质量但推理成本降低约135倍；MR-to-CT中提升了穿平面一致性。

Conclusion: 轻量级切片间轨迹学习是实现高分辨率3D医学图像合成的可行路径。

Abstract: High-resolution 3D medical image generation remains challenging because fully volumetric models are computationally expensive, while efficient 2D slice generators often fail to preserve anatomical consistency across the third dimension. We propose LiFT, a framework for Lifted inter-slice Feature Trajectories that factorizes 3D volume synthesis into per-slice image generation and inter-slice trajectory learning. Rather than modeling the volumetric distribution end-to-end, LiFT treats a volume as an ordered trajectory in feature space, capturing how anatomical structures appear, transform, and disappear across depth. A tri-planar drifting loss aligns the trajectory of generated slices with the trajectories of real volumes, enabling distributional learning over inter-slice progressions in unconditional generation; in paired translation, a bidirectional $z$-context mixer trained against the registered target supplies through-plane coherence while preserving per-slice fidelity. We evaluate LiFT on BraTS 2023 (unconditional and missing-modality MR) and SynthRAD2023 (MR-to-CT). Across these settings, LiFT preserves per-slice quality, approaches the reported cWDM missing-MR reconstruction quality at $\sim$$135\times$ lower inference cost (without formal equivalence testing), and improves through-plane coherence on MR-to-CT relative to a no-mapper ablation, demonstrating that lightweight inter-slice trajectory learning is a viable route to high-resolution 3D medical synthesis.

</details>


### [9] [Learning Long-Term Temporal Dependencies in Photovoltaic Power Output Prediction Through Multi-Horizon Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19074)
*Sumit Laha,Ankit Sharma,Hassan Foroosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出从单步预测转向多步联合预测框架，通过联合优化未来序列值，使深度神经网络更好地捕捉时间依赖关系，从而在不增加计算开销的前提下显著提升太阳光伏功率预测的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球光伏装机容量快速增长（2024年达597 GW），太阳能辐照的间歇性导致电网不稳定，亟需鲁棒的预测模型。现有深度学习方法受限于单架构评估和单步预测，缺乏对多步时间依赖性的建模。

Method: 提出架构无关的多步预测框架，联合优化未来多个时间步的预测值；结合地面天空图像与历史光伏发电数据；通过避免网络过早收敛（权重梯度和滤波器多样性）来捕捉潜在的时间步间依赖关系；在多种深度学习架构上进行对比验证。

Result: 多步预测方法在多种深度学习架构上均显著提升了预测准确性和鲁棒性，且计算开销几乎不增加。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种可扩展且高效的解决方案，有助于提升现代电力系统的韧性。

Abstract: The rapid global expansion of solar photovoltaic (PV) capacity-reaching a record 597 GW in 2024-highlights the urgent need for robust forecasting models to mitigate the grid instability caused by the intermittent nature of solar irradiance. While deep learning-based direct forecasting using ground-based sky images (GSI) has emerged as a dominant approach, existing literature is often constrained by single-architecture evaluations and an exclusive focus on single-horizon (point) prediction. This paper proposes a transition from traditional single-horizon estimation toward a multi-horizon forecasting framework, leading to an architecture-independent improvement in accuracy. We hypothesize and demonstrate experimentally that joint optimization over a sequence of future values allows deep neural networks to better capture latent inter-step temporal dependencies by avoiding precocious convergence of the network in terms of both weight gradients and filter diversity. Leveraging this architecture-independent improvement that integrates sequential sky imagery with historical PV generation data, we evaluate the models' abilities to predict power output across multiple discrete future time steps simultaneously. Our methodology is validated through a comparative analysis across diverse deep learning architectures. The results demonstrate that this multi-horizon approach significantly enhances predictive accuracy and robustness across the entire forecast horizon while maintaining computational parsimony. By achieving superior performance with negligible overhead compared to single-horizon models, this work provides a scalable and efficient solution to improve the resilience of modern power grids.

</details>


### [10] [CRAFT: Critic-Refined Adaptive Key-Frame Targeting for Multimodal Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19075)
*Mahesh Bhosale,Abdul Wasi,Vishvesh Trivedi,Pengyu Yan,Akhil Gorugantu,David Doermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CRAFT 管线，通过动态关键帧选择、ASR 与多语言回退、混合批评者循环迭代验证与修复，在 MAGMaR 2026 和 WikiVideo 数据集上达到领先性能，并公开代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视频问答系统难以从异构视频档案中提取查询相关的证据，且无法为每项声明归因到支持来源，缺乏可验证性和可归因性。

Method: CRAFT 管线包含：(1) 动态关键帧选择；(2) 每视频 ASR 结合多语言回退；(3) 混合批评者循环（UNLI时序蕴含、DeBERTa-v3交叉声明筛选、Llama-3.2-3B裁决器）迭代验证与修复声明；(4) 最终引用合并阶段，每个事实仅输出一次并附带所有支持来源。

Result: 在 MAGMaR 2026 上取得最优平均(0.739)、参考召回(0.810)和引用F1(0.635)；在 WikiVideo 转换的 52 个查询上平均为 0.823；消融实验表明原子声明、ASR 和批评者循环是主要增益来源。

Conclusion: CRAFT 通过声明中心的证据聚合与迭代验证机制，有效提升了多视频问答的准确性和可归因性，且方法具有跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Grounded multi-video question answering over real-world news events requires systems to surface query-relevant evidence across heterogeneous video archives while attributing every claim to its supporting source. We introduce CRAFT (Critic-Refined Adaptive Key-Frame Targeting), a query-conditioned pipeline that combines dynamic keyframe selection, per-video ASR with multilingual fallback, and a hybrid critic loop to iteratively verify and repair claims before consolidation. The pipeline integrates UNLI temporal entailment, DeBERTa-v3 cross-claim screening, and a Llama-3.2-3B adjudicator, with a final citation-merging stage that emits each fact once with all supporting source identifiers. On MAGMaR 2026, CRAFT achieves the best overall average (0.739), reference recall (0.810), and citation F1 (0.635). We further evaluate on a MAGMaR-style conversion of WikiVideo with 52 non-overlapping event queries, where CRAFT also performs strongly (0.823 Avg), showing that its claim-centric evidence aggregation generalizes beyond MAGMaR. Ablations show that atomic claims, ASR, and the critic loop drive the main gains over the vanilla query-conditioned baseline. Code and implementation details are publicly available at https://github.com/bhosalems/CRAFT.

</details>


### [11] [FAGER: Factually Grounded Evaluation and Refinement of Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19111)
*Youngsun Lim,Cusuh Ham,Pin-Yu Chen,Deepti Ghadiyaram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAGER是一个评估文本到图像生成事实正确性的框架，通过构建结构化事实评估量规并结合LLM和VLM，在科学、历史、产品、文化等数据集上优于现有指标，并能无需训练地改进生成结果的事实准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I评估指标主要关注提示词的显式信息对齐，但忽略了隐含的、外部知识相关的或身份定义性的事实要求，无法评估科学知识、历史事实、产品和文化概念等需要事实正确性的场景。

Method: FAGER首先结合LLM提出事实点与参考引导的视觉事实提取及验证，构建结构化事实量规；然后将其转化为问答对，用于VLM评估；最后利用这些评估反馈以无需训练的方式改进T2I输出。

Result: 在科学、历史、产品、文化和知识密集型概念五个数据集上的事实性A/B测试中，FAGER一致优于现有指标。同时，利用FAGER进行无需训练的生成结果优化，在所有数据集上均获得显著的事实性提升。

Conclusion: FAGER有效弥补了现有T2I评估指标在事实正确性方面的不足，既能作为更准确的事实性度量指标，也能以无需训练的方式指导T2I模型生成更符合事实的图像。

Abstract: Existing text-to-image (T2I) evaluation metrics mainly assess whether generated images align with information explicitly stated in the prompt, but often fail to capture factual requirements that are implicit, externally grounded, or identity-defining. As a result, they are not well suited for evaluating factual correctness in prompts involving scientific knowledge, historical facts, products, or culture-specific concepts. We propose FActually Grounded Evaluation and Refinement (FAGER), an agentic framework that evaluates whether generated images correctly reflect visually verifiable facts grounded in or implied by the prompt, while also providing actionable feedback for improvement. FAGER first constructs a structured factual rubric by combining LLM-based fact proposal with reference-guided visual fact extraction and verification, then converts the rubric into question-answer pairs for VLM-based evaluation. To validate FAGER as a factuality metric, we introduce a Factual A/B test, which measures whether a metric prefers factual reference images over corresponding generated images. Across five datasets spanning science, history, products, culture, and knowledge-intensive concepts, FAGER consistently outperforms prior metrics on this test. We further show that FAGER can be used to refine T2I outputs in a fully training-free manner, yielding substantial factuality gains across datasets.

</details>


### [12] [Knowing When Not to Predict: Self Supervised Learning and Abstention for Safer DR Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19133)
*Muskaan Chopra,Lorenz Sparrenberg,Jan H. Terheyden,Rafet Sifa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究自监督预训练时长对医学图像模型校准置信度与基于置信度弃权（选择性预测）的影响，发现预训练能提升选择性预测性能，但准确率饱和后，更长预训练并不一致改善可靠性，建议将预训练时长作为与可靠性相关的设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全性关键的医学筛查任务（如糖尿病视网膜病变分级）中，仅靠下游准确率评估模型不够，模型还需知道何时预测不可靠并弃权。现有SSL研究多关注准确率或AUROC，缺乏对预训练时长如何影响校准置信度和弃权行为的分析。

Method: 在不同数据集和数据量下，对多个SSL预训练检查点采用固定微调协议，评估校准置信度、覆盖率、选择性准确率和选择性宏F1，对比从零训练的效果。

Result: SSL预训练相比从零训练提升了选择性预测性能；但下游准确率饱和后，不同检查点的选择性性能仍有显著变化，且更长预训练并不一致地提升可靠性。

Conclusion: 强调了弃权感知评估的重要性，提出预训练长度应被视为与可靠性相关的重要设计选择，而非仅计算细节。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) is now a standard way to pretrain medical image models, but performance is still mostly judged by downstream accuracy. For safety-critical screening tasks such as diabetic retinopathy grading, this is not enough: a model must also know when its predictions are unreliable and defer uncertain cases for clinical review. In this work, we examine how the length of SSL pretraining influences calibrated confidence and confidence-based abstention. We evaluate multiple SSL checkpoints under a fixed fine-tuning protocol and assess calibrated confidence, coverage, selective accuracy, and selective macro-F1. Across datasets and data regimes, SSL pretraining improves selective prediction compared to training from scratch. Unlike prior SSL studies that primarily evaluate downstream accuracy or AUROC, we analyze how SSL pretraining duration influences confidence behavior under calibrated confidence-based abstention. However, once accuracy saturates, selective performance can still change markedly across checkpoints, and longer pretraining does not consistently improve reliability. These results underscore the importance of abstention-aware evaluation and suggest that pretraining length should be treated as an important reliability-related design choice rather than only a computational detail. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [13] [Towards Data-Efficient Video Pre-training with Frozen Image Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19137)
*Svetlana Orlova,Niccolò Cavagnero,Gijs Dubbelman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索了一种轻量级视频模型训练范式：冻结预训练图像基础模型，仅训练一个循环时序模块处理视频流，实验表明无需大规模视频预训练即可获得强大的时序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基础模型需要大规模视频数据和计算资源进行预训练，而图像基础模型已具备强大的空间表征能力。因此研究能否复用图像模型的空间表征，仅预训练时序推理部分来构建视频模型，从而大幅降低数据和计算成本。

Method: 冻结预训练图像基础模型作为空间编码器，仅训练一个循环时序模块来处理流式视频。该方法避免了端到端视频预训练，只对时序模块进行轻量级训练。

Result: 在多个视频理解任务上的实验表明，无需大规模视频预训练即可获得强大的时序性能。

Conclusion: 该可行性研究证实了通过冻结图像基础模型并仅预训练时序模块来构建循环视频基础模型的潜力，为未来降低视频预训练计算成本的研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Video foundation models achieve strong performance across many video understanding tasks, but typically require large-scale pre-training on massive video datasets, resulting in substantial data and compute costs. In contrast, modern image foundation models already provide powerful spatial representations. This raises an important question: can competitive video models be built by reusing these spatial representations and pre-training only for temporal reasoning? We take initial steps toward exploring a lightweight training paradigm that freezes a pre-trained image foundation model and trains only a recurrent temporal module to process streaming video. By reusing an image foundation model as a spatial encoder, this approach could significantly reduce the amount of video data and compute required compared to end-to-end video pre-training. In this work, we explore the feasibility of this approach before investing in computing for video pre-training. Our empirical findings across multiple video understanding tasks suggest that strong temporal performance can emerge without large-scale video pre-training, motivating future work on recurrent video foundation models obtained by pre-training a temporal module on top of a frozen image foundation model. Code: https://github.com/tue-mps/towards-video-image-frozen .

</details>


### [14] [Efficient coding along the visual hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19155)
*Ananya Passi,Brian S. Robinson,Michael F. Bonner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出深度高效编码模型，仅使用无监督局部统计学习自然图像主变异性，无需标签或反向传播即可构建与人类视觉系统对齐的层次化特征（从边缘到形状），且结合监督微调后在小样本和数据效率上优于传统深度学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生物视觉系统为何能从有限经验中高效学习，检验高效编码原则能否在数据受限条件下构建与人类视觉对齐的深度特征层次。

Method: 开发无监督学习方法，每层仅利用局部统计将输入压缩到自然图像的主变异模式上，不使用标签、任务或反向传播；然后与监督微调结合形成混合学习流程。

Result: 模型特征从边缘、颜色进展到纹理和形状，被人类观察者识别，可预测fMRI脑活动；混合学习在小数据设置下实现更好的脑对齐和更快的类别学习。

Conclusion: 高效编码可能塑造了整个视觉层次上的表征，有助于解释生物视觉的数据效率。

Abstract: Biological visual systems learn from limited experience, unlike deep learning models that rely on millions of training images. What learning principles make this possible? We tested whether efficient coding, the idea that neural representations capture the statistical structure of natural inputs, can build a hierarchy of human-aligned visual features from limited data. We developed an unsupervised learning procedure in which each layer of a deep network compresses its inputs onto the dominant modes of variation in natural images, using only local statistics and no labels, tasks, or backpropagation. This unsupervised procedure yields features that progress from edges and colors to textures and shapes. The features of this deep efficient coding model are readily recognized by human observers and are predictive of image-evoked fMRI responses in human visual cortex. Furthermore, a hybrid learning procedure that combines efficient coding with supervised fine-tuning yields better brain alignment in low-data settings and more rapid category learning. These findings suggest that efficient coding may shape representations across the entire visual hierarchy and help explain the data efficiency of biological vision.

</details>


### [15] [Quantized Machine Learning Models for Medical Imaging in Low-Resource Healthcare Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19207)
*Sumanth Meenan Kanneti,Aryan Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对脑肿瘤MRI分类的多策略模型压缩框架，包括量化感知训练、知识蒸馏（DenseNet-101教师→DenseNet-32学生）和Float16后训练量化。在MobileNetV2流水线上，量化模型达到82.37%验证准确率（全精度基线82.20%），模型大小从35.34MB压缩至5.76MB（6.14倍压缩比），无显著精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在医学图像分析中表现优异，但受限于计算、内存和功耗，在低资源临床环境中部署困难。因此需要模型压缩技术以在资源受限的医疗场景中实现可行的脑肿瘤筛查。

Method: 提出多策略压缩框架：(1) 量化感知训练；(2) 知识蒸馏（DenseNet-101教师→DenseNet-32学生，含低比特后训练量化）；(3) Float16后训练量化（基于MobileNetV2骨干网）。MobileNetV2流水线采用三阶段迁移学习训练，并通过TensorFlow Lite进行Float16量化。

Result: MobileNetV2流水线上，量化模型验证准确率82.37%（全精度基线82.20%），模型大小从35.34MB降至5.76MB，压缩比6.14倍。每类评估表明量化后四类肿瘤的诊断性能均一致保持。DenseNet相关策略的完整实验评估留待未来工作。

Conclusion: 轻量级量化模型可以在资源受限的医疗环境中实现临床可行的脑肿瘤筛查，在显著压缩模型的同时几乎不损失精度。

Abstract: Deep learning models have shown strong performance in medical image analysis, but deploying them in low-resource clinical environments remains difficult due to computational, memory, and power constraints. This paper presents a multi-strategy compression framework for brain tumor classification from MRI, encompassing quantization-aware training, knowledge distillation from a DenseNet-101 teacher to a compact DenseNet-32 student with low-bit post-training quantization, and Float16 post-training quantization on a lightweight MobileNetV2 backbone.
  Using a multi-class brain tumor MRI dataset containing glioma, meningioma, pituitary tumors, and healthy controls, we provide full experimental validation of the MobileNetV2-based pipeline, training the classifier through a three-stage transfer learning process and applying Float16 quantization via TensorFlow Lite. The DenseNet-based distillation and quantization-aware training strategies are described as complementary compression approaches within the framework, with their complete empirical evaluation reserved for future work.
  Experimental results on the MobileNetV2 pipeline show that the quantized model achieves 82.37 percent validation accuracy compared to the 82.20 percent full-precision baseline, reducing model size from 35.34 MB to 5.76 MB, a 6.14x compression ratio with no meaningful accuracy loss. Per-class evaluation confirms that quantization preserves diagnostic performance uniformly across all four tumor categories. These findings demonstrate that lightweight quantized models can deliver clinically viable brain tumor screening in resource-constrained healthcare settings.

</details>


### [16] [D-Convexity: A Unified Differentiable Convex Shape Prior via Quasi-Concavity for Data-driven Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19210)
*Shengzhe Chen,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于准凹性的凸性先验方法，通过可微损失函数将凸形状约束融入端到端分割网络，无需阈值处理，并统一了多种现有凸形状模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸性是许多自然和人造结构的几何先验，但在端到端可训练的分割网络中难以有效施加。

Method: 从网络输出掩码函数的准凹性出发，要求所有超水平集为凸集，导出零阶、一阶和二阶可微不等式约束，并设计凸梯度投影模块（CGPM）集成到分割网络中。

Result: 多个数据集上一致地强制执行凸性并改善形状规则性，优于专用视网膜分割网络和先前的形状感知方法。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了多种现有凸形状模型（离散约束、图割、曲率/水平集方法），提供了连续可微的统一理论框架。

Abstract: Convexity is a fundamental geometric prior that underlies many natural and man-made structures, yet remains challenging to impose effectively in end-to-end trainable segmentation networks. We revisit convexity from a functional perspective and propose a unified, threshold-free convexity prior based on the quasi-concavity of the network's output mask function u. Instead of constraining a single binary segmentation, we require all super-level sets of u to be convex, transforming global shape constraints into local, differentiable inequalities on u and its derivatives. From this principle, we derive zero, first, and second-order characterizations, yielding respectively a local midpoint convexification algorithm, a gradient-based condition linked to supporting hyperplanes, and a sufficient second-order inequality expressed as a quadratic form on the tangent plane. The first and second-order formulations produce a compact convolutional loss that can be densely applied across the image without thresholding. Our quasi-concavity losses integrate seamlessly with modern segmentation networks via the proposed convex gradient projection module (CGPM). They consistently enforce convexity and improve shape regularity across multiple datasets, outperforming networks tailored for retinal segmentation and surpassing previous shape-aware methods. Remarkably, our analysis unifies a wide spectrum of previous convex shape models, from discrete 1-0-1 line constraints and graph-cuts convexity formulations to curvature or signed distance Laplacian based level-set priors, within a single continuous and differentiable framework.

</details>


### [17] [Smartphone-based Circular Plot Sampling for Forest Inventory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19213)
*Su Sun,Jui-Cheng Chiu,Nabin Khanal,Songlin Fei,Yingjie Victor Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅需智能手机和便携支架的轻量化森林测量方法，通过单个行走视频利用单目深度估计、树木分割和SLAM技术，实现对圆形样地内树木的DBH和位置精准测量，在人工林和天然林中分别达到1.51 cm和2.30 cm的平均绝对误差，精度与传统方法相当但成本大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统森林样地测量依赖昂贵的LiDAR系统或繁琐的人工卡尺和罗盘测量，难以在大规模场景中推广。现有方法要么成本高，要么效率低，缺乏一种既经济实惠又操作简便的高精度测量方案。

Method: 提出轻量化智能手机管道：(1) 仅需一部智能手机在便携支架上拍摄单次行走视频；(2) 集成预训练的单目深度估计、树木实例分割与SLAM框架；(3) 联合优化视频序列中的相机轨迹和深度；(4) 通过融合SLAM位姿与分割深度图恢复树木位置和DBH；(5) 利用校准参考长度确定绝对尺度。

Result: 人工林：平均绝对误差1.51 cm (MARE 3.98%)；天然林：平均绝对误差2.30 cm (MARE 5.69%)。在不同起始方向和位置下结果一致，跨视频一致性分析表明树木定位稳定可重复。

Conclusion: 该方法在精度上可与传统测量方法相媲美，同时大幅降低了设备成本和操作复杂度，使得专业研究人员和非专家森林管理者都能在各种操作场景中便捷使用。

Abstract: Circular sample plots are a cornerstone of forest inventory, yet accurate measurement of tree diameter at breast height (DBH) and spatial location within such plots remains challenging. Conventional approaches rely either on costly terrestrial LiDAR systems or labor-intensive manual methods involving calipers and compass bearings, limiting their scalability and accessibility in large scale environments. We present a lightweight, smartphone-based pipeline that enables complete plot sampling based tree measurement from a single walkthrough video, requiring no specialized hardware beyond a consumer smartphone mounted on a portable stand. The proposed method integrates pretrained monocular depth estimation and tree instance segmentation with a simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) framework to jointly refine camera trajectories and depth across the video sequence. Tree positions and DBH estimates are recovered by fusing SLAM-derived camera poses with segmented depth maps, with absolute real-world scale anchored via a calibrated reference length.
  The system was evaluated in both managed forest plots and natural forest plot, achieving a mean absolute error of 1.51 cm (MARE 3.98%) and 2.30 cm (MARE 5.69%) respectively, with consistent performance across varying starting directions and positions. Cross-video consistency analysis further demonstrated stable and reproducible tree localization across measurements initiated from different starting positions. The proposed approach achieves accuracy comparable to established field methods while substantially reducing equipment cost and operational complexity, making it accessible to both professional researchers and non-expert forest managers in diverse operational settings.

</details>


### [18] [Rotation-Aligned Key Channel Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19218)
*Beomseok Kang,Dongwon Jo,Jiwon Song,Donghwee Son,Jae-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RotateK，一种基于旋转的结构化Key通道剪枝框架，通过在线PCA旋转将token依赖的通道重要性对齐到共享低维子空间，实现轻量级头部掩码下的精确剪枝，在固定KV缓存预算下保留更多视觉token，提升VLM推理效率与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM中单张图像编码为数千token导致严重KV缓存压力；现有token剪枝永久丢弃视觉内容，在细粒度感知任务上性能显著下降。因此需要利用特征稀疏性——在固定KV缓存预算下压缩通道维度以保留更多视觉token。

Method: 提出RotateK：1）在线PCA旋转将token依赖的通道重要性对齐到共享低维子空间；2）在该子空间上使用轻量级头部掩码实现结构化剪枝；3）设计融合Triton注意力内核，直接在稀疏通道Key上进行高效解码。

Result: 在两个代表性VLM骨干网络上，RotateK在准确率和解码延迟上均一致优于先前Key通道剪枝方法；在相同KV缓存预算下，联合token-通道剪枝优于纯token剪枝基线。

Conclusion: RotateK解决了Key通道剪枝中token级（表达性强但非结构化和慢）与head级（硬件友好但不够鲁棒）的结构性权衡问题，实现了高效且精确的KV缓存压缩。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models suffer severe KV cache pressure at inference, as a single image often encodes into thousands of tokens. Most existing methods exploit token sparsity through token pruning, but permanently discarding visual content causes substantial degradation on fine-grained perception tasks. This motivates a complementary axis, feature sparsity: under a fixed KV cache budget, compressing the channel dimension preserves more visual tokens at the same memory cost. Prior Key channel pruning methods, however, face a structural trade-off: token-wise channel pruning is expressive but unstructured and slow, while head-wise approach is hardware-friendly but less robust. We resolve this with RotateK, a rotation-based structured Key channel pruning framework. RotateK applies an online PCA-based rotation that aligns token-dependent channel importance into a shared low-dimensional subspace, enabling accurate pruning under lightweight head-wise masks; a fused Triton attention kernel operates directly on sparse-channel Keys for efficient decoding. Experiments on two representative VLM backbones show that RotateK consistently outperforms prior Key channel pruning in both accuracy and decoding latency, while joint token-channel pruning improves over token-only baselines at matched KV cache budgets.

</details>


### [19] [HAVEN: Hierarchically Aligned Multimodal Benchmark for Unified Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19223)
*Mengqi Shi,Haopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HAVEN是一个全新的全粒度（帧、镜头、视频级别）、全模态（视频+文本）层次化对齐的多模态基准数据集与评估套件，用于统一视频理解。通过对现有MLLMs的广泛评测，揭示了文本流畅性与多模态深层理解之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在标准视频任务上表现强劲，但其对复杂叙事的忠实总结与推理能力缺乏有效评估。现有摘要基准将监督信息碎片化（如只针对关键帧、关键镜头或零散的文本摘要），未能捕捉跨模态对齐中固有的层次化结构。

Method: 1. 构建HAVEN数据集：全粒度（帧、镜头、视频级别）且全模态（视频+文本）的架构，包含显式的连续跨模态对齐。2. 提出综合评估套件：涵盖摘要、时序推理、多模态定位和显著性排序四个任务。3. 对当前SOTA MLLMs进行广泛基准测试。

Result: SOTA MLLMs在表面文本流畅性与有根基的多模态理解之间仍存在持续差距，表明模型擅长生成流畅文本但缺乏对视频内容的深层理解。

Conclusion: HAVEN将多模态系统评估从传统QA格式向前推进，提供了一个严格、标准化的测试平台，推动可解释的、层次化的视频理解研究。数据集、基准套件和评估协议均已公开。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) exhibit strong performance on standard video tasks, their ability to faithfully summarize and reason over complex narratives remains poorly evaluated. Existing summarization benchmarks fragment supervision across isolated granularities, such as keyframes, key shots, or disjointed text summaries, failing to capture the inherently hierarchical structure of cross-modal alignment. To address this critical gap, we introduce HAVEN, a hierarchically aligned multimodal benchmark for unified video understanding. HAVEN pioneers a fully granular (frame, shot, and video levels) and fully multimodal (video and text) dataset architecture, complete with explicit, continuous alignment between modalities. Built upon this unified annotation paradigm, we propose a comprehensive evaluation suite spanning summarization, temporal reasoning, multimodal grounding, and saliency ranking. Extensive benchmarking of state-of-the-art MLLMs exposes a persistent gap between surface-level textual fluency and grounded multimodal understanding. Ultimately, HAVEN advances the evaluation of multimodal systems beyond traditional QA formats, offering a rigorous, standardized testbed to drive future research in interpretable, hierarchical video understanding. We publicly release the dataset, benchmark suite, and evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [20] [Robust Mitigation of Age-Dependent Confounding Effects via Sample-Difficulty Decorrelation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19230)
*Nikhil Cherian Kurian,Victor Caquilpan Parra,Abin Shoby,Luke Whitbread,Lyle J. Palmer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对医学图像分类中年龄相关性能差异的鲁棒框架。该方法通过识别并消除年龄导致的标签条件困难趋势中的混杂效应（而非强制年龄不变性），采用Huber加权亲和权重解耦年龄与主导困难趋势，并引入年龄覆盖分数确保优化稳定性。在两个放射学数据集上，该方法在最小化AUC影响的同时减少了年龄相关的真/假阳性差异，并对训练-测试年龄分布偏移具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类中存在年龄相关的性能差异，因为年龄作为混杂因子关联了成像形态与疾病患病率。传统方法强制年龄不变性会抑制有诊断意义的信息，需要更精细的缓解策略。

Method: 1) 预热阶段；2) 以标签为条件建模样本难度与年龄的关系趋势；3) 使用Huber加权亲和权重解耦年龄与主导困难趋势，衰减混杂驱动的捷径；4) 引入年龄覆盖分数，根据小批量年龄方差缩放解耦惩罚，确保有限年龄多样性下的稳定优化。

Result: 在两个放射学数据集上，该方法减少了年龄相关的真阳性和假阳性差异，对AUC影响最小，且对训练-测试年龄分布偏移保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 通过针对虚假的年龄关联趋势而非强制不变性，可以有效缓解年龄导致的混杂效应，在保留诊断信息的同时减少年龄相关性能差异。

Abstract: Age dependent performance disparities in medical image classification often arise because age acts as a confounder, linking imaging morphology with disease prevalence. In practice, disparities can manifest as overdiagnosis at ages where disease prevalence is higher and underdiagnosis at ages where prevalence is lower, and can worsen under train test shifts in the age distribution. Conventional mitigation approaches that enforce strict age invariance may suppress diagnostically meaningful information encoded in age. We therefore propose a robust framework that mitigates the effects of age-dependent confounding by targeting spurious age linked trends rather than enforcing invariance. Following a warm-up phase, we characterize sample difficulty and model its age-dependent trends in a label-conditioned manner. We decorrelate age from dominant age difficulty trends using robust, Huber weighted affinity weights, attenuating confounding-driven shortcuts while preserving clinically meaningful, nonlinear age information. We further introduce an Age Coverage Score that scales the decorrelation penalty by minibatch age variance to ensure stable optimization under limited age diversity. Across two radiology datasets, our approach reduces age dependent true and false positive disparities with minimal AUC impact and remains robust to increasing train test age distribution shifts.

</details>


### [21] [PhyWorld: Physics-Faithful World Model for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19242)
*Pu Zhao,Juyi Lin,Timothy Rupprecht,Arash Akbari,Chence Yang,Rahul Chowdhury,Elaheh Motamedi,Arman Akbari,Yumei He,Chen Wang,Geng Yuan,Weiwei Chen,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhyWorld，通过两阶段后训练（流匹配微调+物理偏好对齐）提升视频生成模型作为世界模拟器的物理忠实性，在标准视频质量基准和专用物理忠实性基准上均取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模视频生成模型虽能生成多样逼真的视觉未来，但作为世界模拟器使用时，缺乏对物理状态的忠实保持和符合基本物理规律的演化能力。

Method: 两阶段后训练：1）流匹配微调（Flow Matching Fine-tuning）提升视频到视频的续接能力，保持稳定的视觉属性和连贯的运动动态；2）基于直接偏好优化（DPO）对齐物理原理，通过物理偏好对引导模型生成更符合物理规律的内容。

Result: VBench平均得分0.769（SOTA基线≤0.756），物理忠实性基准平均得分3.09（最强基线2.99）。

Conclusion: 对大型视频生成模型进行续接训练和物理偏好信号的后训练，可使其成为更有效的Physical AI世界模拟器。

Abstract: World simulators can provide safe and scalable environments for training Physical AI systems before real-world deployment. Large video generation models are emerging as a promising basis for such simulators because they can generate diverse and realistic visual futures. However, using them as world simulators requires physically faithful video continuations, namely, generated videos that preserve the physical state implied by the conditioning input, and evolve in ways consistent with basic physical principles. We propose PhyWorld, a video generation world model designed to produce temporally coherent and physically faithful scene continuations through two-stage post-training. In the first stage, we improve video-to-video continuation with flow matching fine-tuning, encouraging stable visual attributes and coherent motion dynamics across frames. In the second stage, we align generated dynamics with physical principles using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) over physics preference pairs, guiding the model toward outputs with higher physical plausibility. To evaluate PhyWorld, we use both standard video-quality benchmarks and a dedicated physical-faithfulness benchmark with per-law scoring. Experiments show that PhyWorld improves video consistency, achieving an average score of 0.769 on VBench compared with 0.756 or below for state-of-the-art baselines. PhyWorld also improves physical plausibility, reaching an average score of 3.09 on our physical-faithfulness benchmark compared with 2.99 for the strongest baseline. These results suggest that post-training large video generation models with continuation and physics-preference signals can make them more effective world simulators for Physical AI.

</details>


### [22] [Structuring Open-Ended NAS: Semi-Automated Design Knowledge Structuring with LLMs for Efficient Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19247)
*Yuiko Sakuma,Masakazu Yoshimura,Marcel Gröpl,Zitang Sun,Junji Otsuka,Atsushi Irie,Takeshi Ohashi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FairNAD方法，通过半自动化构建模型设计知识模板，利用LLM从论文中提取架构属性，构建丰富搜索空间，并采用多类型变异策略（公平想法采样、帕累托感知变异、LLM迭代变异、细粒度反馈）高效探索该空间，在CIFAR-10/100和ImageNet16-120上分别提升0.84、2.17和2.35个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NAS方法受限于预定义的搜索空间，而LLM辅助的NAS方法虽能实现开放式搜索空间，但因设计想法存在偏差或质量低下而导致探索效率低下。

Method: 首先定义高层结构模板（架构属性模板），再用LLM分析论文填充模板，构建富含结构化设计知识的搜索空间；然后提出FairNAD算法，包含四种变异策略：公平想法采样变异、帕累托感知变异、LLM驱动的迭代变异以及细粒度反馈循环。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet16-120上分别比当前最先进方法高出0.84、2.17和2.35个百分点。

Conclusion: FairNAD通过结构化知识引导与多样化变异策略，有效克服了开放式搜索空间下的低效探索问题，发现性能更优的架构。

Abstract: Current neural architecture search (NAS) methods are often limited by their predefined, restrictive search spaces. While recent large language model (LLM)-assisted NAS methods enable open-ended search spaces, they often suffer from inefficient exploration due to biased or low-quality design ideas. To address these issues, we propose to semi-automatically structure model design knowledge to guide the search process. Our approach first defines a high-level structural template of architectural attributes. An LLM then populates this template by analyzing papers, creating a rich and diverse search space that embodies this structured design knowledge. To efficiently explore this vast space, we introduce FairNAD, using a multi-type mutation that enables broad exploration through mutation with fair idea sampling, Pareto-aware mutation, LLM-driven iterative mutation, and a fine-grained feedback loop. We demonstrate the effectiveness of FairNAD in discovering high-performing architectures that yield 0.84, 2.17, and 2.35 points improvement on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet16-120, respectively, compared to current state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [23] [Distribution Matching Distillation without Fake Score Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19256)
*Youngjoong Kim,Deokyeong Lee,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FSF-DMD方法，通过用生成器自身的伪速度替代辅助假分数网络，在流图生成器中实现无额外网络的分发匹配蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMD方法依赖额外的假分数网络来追踪生成分布，带来了内存和更新开销。本文研究在流图生成器结构下能否避免这个显式追踪器。

Method: 提出FSF-DMD，利用流图生成器的端点伪速度作为假速度估计的代理，替代辅助假分数网络。推导了实用目标函数，扩展了流图一致的后向模拟，并引入自教师变体用于从头训练。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K 256×256上，FSF-DMD改进了流图基线，在流图初始化设置下达到了比DMD2更低的FID，在流匹配初始化和从头训练下也有效。

Conclusion: 流图生成器自身的端点伪速度可以替代额外的假分数网络，实现更高效的DMD训练。

Abstract: Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) provides an effective distribution-level correction for few-step generation, while relying on an auxiliary fake-score network to track the evolving generative distribution. Recent work combines DMD-style objectives with flow-map generators to exploit both forward-divergence training and reverse-divergence correction. The fake-score estimator remains an additional component with memory and update overhead. In this work, we study whether this explicit tracker can be avoided when the generator itself has a flow-map structure. We propose Fake-Score-network-Free DMD (FSF-DMD), a DMD formulation for flow-map generators that replaces the auxiliary fake-score estimator with a generator-induced pseudo-velocity surrogate. The key observation is that the endpoint pseudo-velocity of a flow-map generator provides a tractable proxy for fake-velocity estimation, allowing the generator itself to supply the reverse-divergence signal. Building on this observation, we derive a practical objective, extend it with flow-map-consistent backward simulation, and introduce a self-teacher variant for training from scratch. In our ImageNet-1K $256 \times 256$ experiments, FSF-DMD improves flow-map baselines, reaches lower FID than the listed DMD2 comparisons in the flow-map-initialized setting, and remains effective under flow-matching initialization and training from scratch.

</details>


### [24] [FPED: A Functional-Network Prior-Guided Mixture-of-Experts Framework for Interpretable Brain Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19279)
*Yudan Ren,Pengcheng Shi,Zihan Ma,Xiaowei He,Xiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FPED框架，利用功能网络先验和混合专家模型进行fMRI视觉图像重建，在保持神经科学可解释性的同时实现高效语义重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将fMRI信号扁平化为1D向量直接映射到CLIP潜在空间，破坏了大脑固有的网络拓扑结构，且忽视了不同功能网络对高级视觉语义的协同贡献。

Method: 提出FPED框架，将不同功能脑网络建模为专门的专家模块，通过自适应路由机制捕获各网络对视觉语义理解的互补贡献，实现结构化、可解释的网络级表示学习。

Result: 仅用0.68B参数即达到高度竞争力的语义重建性能，学习到的路由动态揭示了功能脑网络与模态特异性语义处理之间的生物学对应关系。

Conclusion: 脑网络感知的专家建模是连接神经解码与生物启发式人工智能的有前景方向。

Abstract: Visual image reconstruction from functional Magnetic Resonance Imaging (fMRI) is a fundamental task in brain decoding, providing a crucial pathway for understanding human perceptual mechanisms and developing advanced brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). However, most current methods simply flatten fMRI signals from localized visual cortices into one-dimensional (1D) vectors, mapping them directly into latent spaces such as that of Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP). This paradigm not only disrupts the inherent network topology of the brain-leading to limited neuroscientific interpretability-but also overlooks the synergistic contributions of other distributed functional networks in processing high-level visual semantics. To address these limitations, we propose FPED, a Functional-Network Prior-Guided Mixture of Experts (MoE) framework for interpretable brain decoding. FPED explicitly models different functional brain networks as specialized experts and employs adaptive routing to capture their complementary contributions to visual semantic understanding. Unlike conventional homogeneous decoding paradigms, our framework incorporates neurobiologically grounded priors to enable structured and interpretable network-level representation learning. Experimental results demonstrate that FPED achieves highly competitive semantic reconstruction performance with only 0.68B parameters. The learned routing dynamics reveal biologically meaningful correspondence between functional brain networks and modality-specific semantic processing, providing transparent neuroscientific interpretability. This suggests that brain network-aware expert modeling is a promising direction for bridging neural decoding and biologically inspired artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [25] [What Makes Synthetic Data Effective in Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19289)
*Jinjin Zhang,Xiefan Guo,Yizhou Jin,Nan Zhou,Di Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了扩散模型生成图像在视觉分割任务中的效用，发现密集场景构图和高实例保真度的合成图像能显著提升分割性能，并提出了一个模型无关的统一框架SENSE，在多个数据集上验证了其有效性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散模型能生成逼真的图像，但其在复杂视觉分割任务中的潜力尚未被充分探索。作者希望通过系统分析来揭示哪些因素决定了合成图像对分割任务的有效性，并在此基础上构建一个能充分利用合成数据提升分割性能的统一框架。

Method: 首先系统分析扩散模型合成图像的特性，发现密集场景构图和细粒度实例保真度是关键因素；然后提出SENSE框架，该框架灵活、可扩展、模型无关，兼容多种架构（如DPT和Mask2Former），并能适配不同参数规模的模型。

Result: 在Cityscapes、COCO和ADE20K数据集上进行了大量实验，验证了SENSE框架的有效性和泛化能力，表明合成数据能显著提升分割性能。

Conclusion: 扩散模型生成的合成图像在视觉分割任务中具有实用价值，SENSE框架能够灵活利用合成数据提升多种架构的分割性能，且具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Driven by rapid advances in large-scale generative models, synthetic data has emerged as a promising solution for visual understanding. While modern diffusion models achieve remarkable photorealistic image synthesis, their potential in complex visual segmentation tasks remains underexplored. In this work, we conduct a systematic analysis of synthetic images from state-of-the-art diffusion models to uncover the factors governing their utility. In particular, synthetic images characterized by dense scene composition and fine instance fidelity demonstrate distinctive benefits, yielding significantly more discriminative spatial representations. Building on these insights, we propose SENSE, a unified framework that leverages flexible and scalable synthetic data to substantially enhance segmentation performance. Notably, SENSE is model-agnostic, compatible with diverse architectures (e.g., DPT and Mask2Former), and scales effectively across models with varying parameter capacities. Extensive experiments on Cityscapes, COCO, and ADE20K validate the effectiveness and generalization capability of our approach. Code is available at https://github.com/zhang0jhon/SENSE.

</details>


### [26] [iGSP:Implicit Gradient Subspace Projection for Efficient Continual Learning of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19301)
*Xuezhi Cui,Dongbo Zhou,Wang Guo,Zeyuan Wang,Ziyu Li,Gaozhi Zhou,Xian Li,Ling Zhao,Wentao Yang,Chao Tao,Haifeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出iGSP框架，通过隐式梯度子空间投影实现视觉-语言模型的高效持续学习，在MTIL基准上达到SOTA精度，同时减少42.7%可训练参数和86.9%总参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法面临两个问题：为每个任务分配独立模块导致参数爆炸；基于视觉相似性的共享机制错误地将表面视觉相似等同于底层对齐一致性，导致负迁移且无法利用跨任务的对齐复用。

Method: iGSP框架将对齐共享视为低秩子空间内优化轨迹重叠的几何问题。分为两个阶段：1) 子空间识别阶段：通过MoE路由器的早期收敛建立子空间基，进行基预扩展引入候选专家，施加子空间约束正则化隐式投影新任务梯度，利用路由概率作为梯度流指标剪枝冗余维度；2) 正交子空间微调阶段：固定结构基并移除正则化，快速拟合任务特定残差损失。

Result: 在MTIL基准上达到SOTA精度，平均可训练参数减少42.7%，最终总参数减少86.9%。

Conclusion: 实验证明iGSP在持续学习场景下兼顾了知识复用与任务特异性，验证了将对齐共享建模为几何子空间问题的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models require efficient adaptation to continually emerging downstream tasks. While Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning mitigates catastrophic forgetting, assigning isolated modules per task leads to parameter explosion. Conversely, recent similarity-driven sharing mechanisms falsely equate superficial visual similarity with underlying alignment consistency. This fundamental mismatch triggers severe negative transfer between visually similar but logically distinct tasks and fails to exploit alignment reuse across visually diverse ones. We argue thatalignment sharing is fundamentally a geometric problem of overlapping optimization trajectories within shared low-rank subspaces. Grounded in this insight, we propose iGSP, a novel framework that achieves efficient adaptation via implicit gradient subspace projection. Leveraging the early convergence of MoE routers to establish the subspace basis, iGSP bifurcates the adaptation process into two phases. First, the Subspace Identification phase introduces candidate experts via basis pre-expansion, applies a novel subspace-constrained regularization to implicitly project new task gradients onto the historical subspace, and precisely prunes redundant dimensions by treating routing probabilities as gradient flow indicators, ultimately to maximize knowledge reuse. Second, the Orthogonal Subspace Fine-Tuning phase fixes this structural basis and removes the regularization to rapidly fit the task-specific residual loss. Extensive experiments on the MTIL benchmark demonstrate that iGSP achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while significantly improving training efficiency, reducing the average trainable parameters by 42.7\% compared to current SOTA methods, and decreasing the final total parameters by 86.9\% relative to counterparts. The source code is available at https://github.com/GeoX-Lab/iGSP.

</details>


### [27] [MMGS: 10$\times$ Compressed 3DGS through Optimal Transport Aggregation based on Multi-view Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19304)
*Beizhen Zhao,Sicheng Yu,Ziran Yin,Dongxu Shen,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于最优传输的3D高斯全局几何分布匹配框架，通过贡献度排名、聚合和密化算子，仅用10%的图元实现比原始3DGS更好的渲染质量，并提速10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS存在大量冗余图元导致开销巨大，现有压缩方法依赖局部采样或固定阈值，难以在减少冗余和保持高质量渲染之间取得平衡。

Method: (1)多视角3D高斯贡献度排名机制，基于几何一致性而非局部启发式筛选图元；(2)基于全局最优传输(OT)的聚合算法，合并冗余图元同时保持底层几何；(3)基于OT的密化算子，维持高斯分布特性实现稳定优化。

Result: 仅用10%的图元和10倍训练加速，即可达到SOTA渲染质量。

Conclusion: 将高斯优化形式化为全局几何分布匹配问题，通过OT实现冗余消除与几何保持的统一，显著提升了3DGS的效率与质量。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has revolutionized 3D reconstruction, it suffers from significant overhead due to massive redundant primitives. Existing compression methods typically rely on local sampling or fixed pruning thresholds, which often struggle to balance redundancy reduction with high-fidelity rendering. To address this, we propose a novel framework that formulates Gaussian optimization as a global geometric distribution matching problem. Specifically, our approach integrates three components: (1) we introduce a multi-view 3D Gaussian contribution ranking mechanism that filters primitives using geometric consistency instead of local heuristics; (2) we propose a global Optimal Transport (OT)-based aggregation algorithm that merges redundant primitives while preserving the underlying geometry; and (3) we design an OT-based densification operator that maintains the Gaussian's distributional properties for stable optimization. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality with only \textbf{10$\%$} primitives and \textbf{10$\times$} accelerated training speeds compared to vanilla 3DGS.

</details>


### [28] [MetaRA: Metamorphic Robustness Assessment for Multimodal Large Language Model-based Visual Question Answering Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19307)
*Quanxing Xu,Yuhao Tian,Ling Zhou,Xian Zhong,Xiaohua Huang,Rubing Huang,Chia-Wen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MetaRA，一种基于蜕变测试的框架，通过系统生成图像-问题的受控变体来评估多模态大模型在VQA任务中的鲁棒性，揭示传统准确率指标无法捕捉的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VQA评估依赖静态数据集和准确率指标，无法有效衡量模型的鲁棒性、一致性和泛化能力，需要更系统的测试方法来发现隐藏的脆弱点。

Method: 基于蜕变测试（MT）原理，定义多种蜕变关系（MRs），生成图像-问题输入的受控变体，对多个MLLM-based VQA模型进行系统性鲁棒性评估。

Result: 发现了模型的多类失败模式：对语言扰动的敏感、过度依赖表面视觉线索，以及多模态推理中的深层缺陷。MetaRA提供了比传统准确率指标更丰富的诊断信息。

Conclusion: 强调系统性鲁棒性评估在VQA中的必要性，将蜕变评估定位为一种可扩展、模型无关的方法，助力可信多模态AI的发展。

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA), as the representative multimodal task, serves as a key benchmark for evaluating the reasoning capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, existing evaluations largely rely on static datasets and accuracy-based metrics, which fail to capture robustness, consistency, and generalization. Inspired by Metamorphic Testing (MT), we propose Metamorphic Robustness Assessment (MetaRA), a testing framework that employs Metamorphic Relations (MRs) to systematically probe vulnerabilities in MLLM-based VQA systems. MetaRA generates controlled variations of image-question inputs based on specific MRs and evaluates models across diverse conditions. Applying MetaRA to multiple MLLM-based VQA models across different tasks reveals nuanced failure patterns, including sensitivity to linguistic perturbations, over-reliance on superficial visual cues, and deeper weaknesses in multimodal reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate that MetaRA provides richer diagnostic insights than conventional accuracy metrics, exposing failure modes that remain hidden under standard benchmarks. Overall, this work highlights the need for systematic robustness evaluation in VQA and positions metamorphic assessment as a scalable, model-agnostic approach toward trustworthy multimodal AI.

</details>


### [29] [SWEET: Sparse World Modeling with Image Editing for Embodied Task Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19319)
*Yiren Song,Yihan Wang,Xiyao Deng,Zhuoran Yan,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SWEET框架，利用图像编辑模型（而非昂贵的视频生成）作为稀疏视觉世界模型，为机器人操作任务逐步生成关键帧，并通过扩散动作预测器转化为可执行动作，在DROID和RoboMimic上取得良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集视频生成计算成本高且对于操作任务往往不必要，因为任务进展仅需少量任务相关的视觉状态即可概括。作者希望探索图像编辑模型能否作为一种高效的稀疏视觉世界模型替代方案。

Method: 1) 对比Wan2.2视频生成模型与FLUX-Kontext图像编辑模型，发现后者在关键帧保真度和推理成本上更优。2) 提出SWEET框架：单次稀疏视觉规划，通过渐进式图像编辑生成任务相关关键帧序列，结合语言指令和箭头空间引导。3) 使用目标条件扩散动作预测器将相邻关键帧转化为可执行动作。4) 引入混合训练策略，用过滤后的编辑目标减少真实与编辑子目标之间的不匹配。

Result: 在DROID和RoboMimic数据集上，SWEET在已知和未知场景下均改进了关键帧预测效果，并实现了从关键帧规划到机器人动作执行的完整流程。

Conclusion: 图像编辑模型是具身视觉预测中一个被低估但有前景的研究方向，能够以更低成本实现可靠的任务级关键帧预测和机器人操控。

Abstract: Visual prediction has emerged as a promising paradigm for embodied control, where future observations are generated and then translated into actions. However, dense video generation is computationally expensive and often unnecessary for many manipulation tasks, whose progress can be summarized by a small number of task-relevant visual states. In this work, we study whether image editing models can serve as sparse visual world models for robot manipulation by predicting task-level future states without dense video rollout. We first conduct a controlled comparison between the video generation model Wan2.2 and the image editing model FLUX-Kontext under the same robotic data setting, and find that image editing produces more reliable task-level keyframes with better visual fidelity and substantially lower inference cost. Motivated by this observation, we propose SWEET, a one-shot sparse visual planning framework that progressively generates a sequence of task-relevant manipulation keyframes through successive image editing, conditioned on language instructions and optional arrow-based spatial guidance. A goal-conditioned diffusion action predictor then converts adjacent imagined keyframes into executable action chunks. To reduce the mismatch between real and edited visual subgoals, we further introduce a mixed-training strategy with filtered edited targets. Experiments on DROID and RoboMimic show that SWEET improves keyframe prediction across seen and unseen scenes and enables a full pipeline from sequential keyframe planning to executable robot actions, suggesting that image editing is a promising and underexplored direction for embodied visual prediction.

</details>


### [30] [TextAlign: Preference Alignment for Text Rendering with Hierarchical Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19320)
*Mingxuan Cui,Jingpu Yang,Fengxian Ji,Qian Jiang,Zhecheng Shi,Jiaming Wang,Zirui Song,Fajri Koto,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TextAlign提出了一种非侵入式的后训练偏好对齐框架，通过层次化VLM奖励机制（全局、单词、字形三级）将文本渲染缺陷转化为标量偏好信号，在不改变生成器架构的前提下，显著提升了FLUX.1-dev和Z-Image-Turbo等模型的OCR文本准确率，性能超越SD3.5、Qwen-Image等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成模型在忠实渲染文本方面存在持续弱点，而此前的方法往往依赖特定架构模块或编码器修改，导致部署复杂且难以跨基础模型推广。作者希望提出一种可扩展的、不改变生成器架构的解决方案。

Method: 将文本渲染建模为后训练偏好对齐问题，提出TextAlign框架。核心是构建一个层次化视觉语言模型（VLM）奖励函数，在全局、单词和字形三个层级分解渲染错误，将二元缺陷判断转化为标量偏好信号，该信号同时支持GRPO和DPO两种偏好优化方式。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev和Z-Image-Turbo上实验，OCR文本准确率获得一致提升，且不损害通用生成质量。在对比SD3.5、Qwen-Image、AnyText、TextDiffuser等基线时表现优越。

Conclusion: 奖励设计是一种可扩展的替代方案，可以替代模型重新设计来提升文本渲染能力。

Abstract: Faithful text rendering remains a persistent weakness of large text-to-image generative models, as it requires both semantic instruction following and fine-grained glyph-level structure. Prior methods often improve this ability through architecture-specific modules or encoder modifications, which complicate deployment across foundation models. We study text rendering as a post-training preference-alignment problem and propose TextAlign, a non-invasive framework that keeps the generator architecture unchanged. The key component is a hierarchical vision-language model (VLM)-based reward that decomposes rendering errors into global, word, and glyph levels, then converts binary defect judgments into a scalar preference signal. The resulting signal supports both Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and Z-Image-Turbo show consistent gains in OCR-based text accuracy without degrading general generation quality. Compared with strong foundation and text-rendering baselines, including SD3.5, Qwen-Image, AnyText, and TextDiffuser, these results indicate that reward design offers a scalable alternative to model redesign for improving text rendering.

</details>


### [31] [DynaTok: Temporally Adaptive and Positional Bias-Aware Token Compression for Video-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19322)
*Minyoung Park,Taehun Kong,Sangjun Ahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynaTok 是一种无需训练、自适应且能缓解位置偏见的视频token压缩框架，通过时空双维度预算分配，在90% token缩减率下仍保持95%以上的基线准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大语言模型处理长序列时视觉token数量巨大，计算成本高昂；现有训练无关的token压缩方法仅依赖注意力值作为重要性指标，忽略了位置偏差且仅关注短时局部性，导致token使用效率低下。

Method: 提出DynaTok框架，包含两个模块：1) 时序预算分配（TBA）：通过轻量级指数移动平均（EMA）记忆动态为冗余帧分配更少token、为新异帧分配更多token；2) 空间预算分配（SBA）：利用激活注意力图选择空间多样且语义重要的特征，并通过空间记忆减少已选区域的冗余，缓解位置偏差。

Result: 在MVBench、LongVideoBench、MLVU、VideoMME四个VideoQA基准上，即使token缩减90%，仍保持超过95%的基线准确率，性能优于现有训练无关方法。

Conclusion: DynaTok为高效鲁棒的视频推理提供了有原则的基础框架，为未来Video-LLMs实现实时流式视频理解铺平了道路。

Abstract: Recent advances in Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have greatly expanded multimodal reasoning capabilities. However, the massive number of visual tokens extracted from long video sequences incurs prohibitive computational costs, limiting their deployment in real-world scenarios. Existing training-free token compression methods select tokens based on attention magnitude as a proxy for semantic importance, but often overlook positional bias and rely only on short-term temporal locality, leading to redundant spatio-temporal coverage and inefficient token usage. We present DynaTok, a training-free, temporally adaptive and bias-aware token compression framework that allocates token budgets across both temporal and spatial dimensions. Through a lightweight exponential moving average (EMA) memory, the Temporal Budget Allocation (TBA) module dynamically assigns fewer tokens to redundant frames and more to novel frames, capturing long-term temporal variation. The Spatial Budget Allocation (SBA) module complements this by selecting spatially diverse and semantically important features using activation-based attention maps, while leveraging a spatial memory to reduce redundancy from previously selected regions and mitigate positional bias. DynaTok integrates seamlessly with existing Video-LLMs such as LLaVA-OneVision and LLaVA-Video without retraining, and effectively preserves semantic coverage under aggressive compression. Experiments on four representative VideoQA benchmarks-MVBench, LongVideoBench, MLVU, and VideoMME-show that DynaTok retains over 95% of baseline accuracy even with a 90% token reduction, surpassing recent training-free approaches. These results demonstrate that DynaTok provides a principled foundation for efficient and robust video reasoning, paving the way toward real-time streaming video understanding with future Video-LLMs.

</details>


### [32] [RE-VLM: Event-Augmented Vision-Language Model for Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19329)
*Hanqing Liu,Mingjie Liu,Luoping Cui,Endian Lin,Donghong Jiang,Chuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RE-VLM是首个联合RGB图像和事件流的双流视觉语言模型，通过并行编码器和渐进训练策略，在常规和极端条件下均实现鲁棒的场景理解。为解决标注数据稀缺问题，提出图驱动管道自动生成场景图及相应的描述文本和问答对，并构建了两个新数据集。实验表明RE-VLM在描述生成和VQA任务上优于同量级的RGB-only和event-only模型，尤其在挑战性场景中优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉语言模型在低光、高动态范围或快速运动等恶劣条件下表现不佳，因为标准RGB图像在这些环境中质量严重下降。事件相机作为一种互补模态，能够异步记录像素亮度变化，具有高时间分辨率和宽动态范围，能在帧失效时保留运动线索。作者旨在通过融合RGB和事件两种模态，实现跨正常与挑战性场景的鲁棒视觉语言理解。

Method: 提出RE-VLM双流架构：并行RGB编码器和事件编码器分别提取两种模态的特征，并通过渐进训练策略将异构视觉特征与语言对齐。为解决RGB-事件-文本三元组标注稀缺问题，设计了基于图的自动管道：将同步的RGB-事件流转换为可验证的场景图，再从中合成描述文本和问答对。同时构建了两个新数据集PEOD-Chat（光照挑战场景）和RGBE-Chat（多样化场景）。

Result: 在描述生成和VQA基准测试上，RE-VLM在参数规模相当的情况下，一致优于最先进的RGB-only和event-only模型，在挑战性条件下性能提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 事件增强的视觉语言模型能在广泛真实场景中实现鲁棒的视觉语言理解，验证了融合事件模态的有效性。代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Conventional vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to interpret scenes captured under adverse conditions (e.g., low light, high dynamic range, or fast motion) because standard RGB images degrade in such environments. Event cameras provide a complementary modality: they asynchronously record per-pixel brightness changes with high temporal resolution and wide dynamic range, preserving motion cues where frames fail. We propose RE-VLM, the first dual-stream vision-language model that jointly leverages RGB images and event streams for robust scene understanding across both normal and challenging conditions. RE-VLM employs parallel RGB and event encoders together with a progressive training strategy that aligns heterogeneous visual features with language. To address the scarcity of RGB-Event-Text supervision, we further propose a graph-driven pipeline that converts synchronized RGB-Event streams into verifiable scene graphs, from which we synthesize captions and question-answer (QA) pairs. To develop and evaluate RE-VLM, we construct two datasets: PEOD-Chat, targeting illumination-challenged scenes, and RGBE-Chat, covering diverse scenarios. On captioning and VQA benchmarks, RE-VLM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art RGB-only and event-only models with comparable parameter counts, with particularly large gains under challenging conditions. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of event-augmented VLMs in achieving robust vision-language understanding across a wide range of real-world environments. Code and datasets are available at https://github.com/bupt-ai-cz/RE-VLM.

</details>


### [33] [Selective, Regularized, and Calibrated: Harnessing Vision Foundation Models for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19340)
*Junyuan Ma,Xunzhi Xiang,Wenbin Li,Qi Fan,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HERA框架，通过层级选择-正则化-校准流水线，在不重新训练的情况下，仅微调不到2.7%的参数，即可让视觉基础模型适应跨域小样本分割任务，在多个基准上超越SOTA达4.1 mIoU以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型在跨域小样本分割中面临两大挑战：(1) 每类标注样本极少，模型容易过拟合；(2) 目标域分布偏移在预训练中未被充分覆盖，导致跨域不一致性和层间敏感性。现有方法难以在有限标签和域偏移下有效适应。

Method: 提出HERA三阶段框架：①层级选择（HLS）：基于数据依赖的示例迁移风险自适应选择最具信息量的VFM层；②先验引导正则化（PGR）：在选定表示上正则化交互，产生结构良好的局部信号；③像素级自适应校准（PAC）：结合选定表示与细化交互图校准像素级预测。整个流程仅微调不到2.7%的参数。

Result: 在多个CD-FSS基准上，HERA相比SOTA提升了超过4.1 mIoU，且测试时仅微调极少量参数。

Conclusion: HERA通过层级选择-正则化-校准策略，有效解决了VFM在跨域小样本分割中的过拟合和域偏移问题，实现了无需源数据重新训练的高效域适应。

Abstract: Vision foundation models (VFMs) have achieved strong performance across various vision tasks. However, it still remains challenging to apply VFMs for cross-domain few-shot segmentation (CD-FSS), which segments objects of novel classes under domain shifts using only a few labeled exemplars. The challenge is mainly driven by two factors: (1) limited labeled exemplars per novel class relative to the scale of VFM pre-training, making the model prone to overfitting during retraining, and (2) target-domain shifts underrepresented during pre-training, inducing cross-domain inconsistency and layer-wise sensitivity. To address these issues, we propose Hierarchical Exemplar Representation Adaptation (HERA), a three-stage select-regularize-calibrate VFM-based segmentation framework that learns effectively from limited labels and adapts to novel domains without source-data retraining. We first design Hierarchical Layer Selection (HLS) to adaptively identify the most informative VFM layer using a data-dependent Exemplar Transfer Risk (ETR) computed for each candidate layer. Then, Prior-Guided Regularization (PGR) regularizes interactions on the selected representation, yielding well-structured local signals for the subsequent stage. Furthermore, Pixelwise Adaptive Calibration (PAC) combines the selected representation with the refined interaction maps to calibrate pixel-wise predictions, producing consistent masks. Together, these stages form a hierarchical select-regularize-calibrate pipeline that guides frozen VFM features in new domains while fine-tuning less than 2.7% of parameters at test time. Extensive experiments show that HERA surpasses the state of the art by more than 4.1 mIoU across multiple CD-FSS benchmarks.

</details>


### [34] [Semantic-Enriched Latent Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19342)
*Tianrun Xu,Yue Sun,Qixun Wang,Jingyi Lu,Yuan Wang,Tianren Zhang,Longteng Guo,Fengyun Rao,Jing Lyu,Feng Chen,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SLVR 是一个两阶段框架，通过属性级语义监督和多查询策略优化，增强潜在空间中的视觉推理能力，实验表明在鲁棒性和语义一致性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在空间推理方法依赖视觉监督，导致潜在表示语义丰富度不足，难以支持多样的区域级推理任务。

Method: 两阶段学习框架：(1) 在细粒度属性监督下学习语义增强的区域中心潜在表示；(2) 提出多查询组相对策略优化（M-GRPO），对齐同一区域的多个查询对应的潜在表示。同时构建了 SLV-Set 数据集（约40万属性标注+80万问答样本）和 SV-QA 基准。

Result: SLVR 在鲁棒性和语义一致性方面优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过语义增强的潜在表示和多查询对齐策略，SLVR 有效提升了潜在空间视觉推理的质量，证明了引入属性级语义监督的必要性。

Abstract: Multimodal latent-space reasoning aims to replace explicit thinking with images by performing visual reasoning directly in a compact latent space. However, existing approaches largely rely on visual supervision and produce latent representations that lack sufficient semantic richness, limiting their ability to support diverse region-level reasoning tasks. In this work, we introduce Semantic-Enriched Latent Visual Reasoning (SLVR), a two-stage learning framework that enriches latent representations with attribute-level visual semantics and aligns them with diverse reasoning objectives. In the first stage, SLVR learns semantically enriched region-centric latents under fine-grained attribute supervision. In the second stage, we design Multi-query Group Relative Policy Optimization (M-GRPO) to align latent representations across multiple queries grounded in the same region. To support this framework, we construct SLV-Set, comprising approximately 400K region-level attribute annotations and 800K multi-query question answering samples, and introduce SV-QA, a benchmark that evaluates latent reasoning under semantic variation. Experiments demonstrate that SLVR improves the robustness and semantic consistency of latent visual reasoning compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [35] [MAM-CLIP: Vision-Language Pretraining on Mammography Atlases for BI-RADS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19359)
*Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAM-CLIP，利用乳腺摄影图谱的图像-文本对进行对比学习预训练，再将视觉编码器微调用于BI-RADS评分预测，在标注数据稀缺时提升显著（+1%~+14% F1），并发现图谱文本信息比同等数量标注数据更有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺X光图像复杂度高，仅靠图像标签训练分类模型性能有限；且放射科医生之间对BI-RADS评分判读存在分歧，亟需利用更丰富的文本信息（如影像描述）来提升模型对乳腺影像的理解能力。

Method: 收集2313张乳腺X光图像及其对应描述文本，使用预训练PubMedBERT作为语言编码器，通过对比学习训练图像-文本对，使视觉编码器从文本中吸收丰富信息；再在两组数据集上对视觉编码器进行微调，用于BI-RADS评分预测。

Result: 在3类平均F1分数上，相比无预训练模型提升+1%~+14%（40K样本时+1%，1K样本时+14%）；且2000对图像-文本比2000个标注样本对标签预测更有效（超过10K训练样本时平均提升+1.1%）。

Conclusion: 证明了乳腺摄影图谱中的文本信息对提升模型性能具有重要价值，尤其在标注数据稀缺场景下优势显著；同时开源了代码、模型权重和TEKNOFEST数据集预处理后的图像。

Abstract: Deep learning methods have demonstrated promising results in predicting BI-RADS scores from mammography images. However, the interpretation of these images can vary, leading to discrepancies even among radiologists. Given the inherent complexity of mammograms, training classification models solely on image labels often yields limited performance. To address this challenge, we curated 2313 mammogram images and their corresponding captions from two mammography atlases. Our proposed approach employs a multi-modal model that uses a pretrained PubMedBERT as the language component. By training this model on image-text pairs with contrastive learning, we enable the vision encoder to absorb the rich information contained in the captions, thereby improving its understanding of mammography findings. We then fine-tune the vision encoder on two datasets for BI-RADS prediction, achieving superior performance compared with models trained without this pretraining, particularly when labeled samples are scarce. The improvement in the 3-class average F1 score ranges from +1% to +14%: a +1% increase with 40K training samples, and a +14% increase with 1K samples. Furthermore, our experiments reveal that 2K image-text pairs from mammography atlases can be more informative than 2K labeled samples for label prediction, with an average margin of +1.1% when more than 10K training samples are available. Overall, our work provides a vision-language model for mammography and highlights the value of textual information from mammography atlases. In addition, we publicly release preprocessed mammography images of the TEKNOFEST dataset. The training code, pre-trained model weights, data extraction scripts, and the released dataset are publicly available at: https://github.com/igulluk/MAM-CLIP

</details>


### [36] [Scalable, Energy-Efficient Optical-Neural Architecture for Multiplexed Deepfake Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19360)
*Parnian Ghapandar Kashani,Shiqi Chen,Aydogan Ozcan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合数字-模拟深度伪造视频检测框架，将轻量级数字前端与空间复用的光学解码后端结合，能在单次光学传播中并行处理15个以上视频流，在Celeb-DF数据集上达到97.79%的平均检测准确率，兼具高吞吐量、能效和对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的深度伪造检测方法依赖计算密集且高能耗的推理算法，限制了其可扩展性，亟需一种高效、可信赖的检测系统。

Method: 提出混合数字-模拟框架：轻量级数字前端提取特征，结合可编程空间光调制器实现空间复用的光学解码后端，在单次光学传播中并行处理15个以上视频流进行大规模并行模拟推理。

Result: 在Celeb-DF数据集上，15个视频并行单次光学推理下，平均检测准确率97.79%，灵敏度99.86%，特异性95.72%；对视频退化、噪声、压缩、对准误差和黑盒对抗攻击均表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将光学计算集成到AI推理中，可以在吞吐量、能效和对抗鲁棒性这三个纯数字系统难以兼得的方面同时获得提升。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of AI-generated visual media has created an urgent need for efficient, trustworthy deepfake detection systems. However, existing deep learning-based detection methods rely on computationally intensive and energy-demanding inference algorithms, limiting their scalability. Here, we present a hybrid digital-analog deepfake video detection framework that combines a lightweight digital front-end with a spatially multiplexed optical decoding back-end for massively parallel analog inference through a programmable spatial light modulator. By simultaneously processing 15 or more video streams within a single optical propagation pass, the system enables high-throughput and accurate video-level authenticity prediction at reduced computational cost compared with purely digital methods. We validated this hybrid deepfake video processor using different datasets spanning classical face-swapping, real-world deepfake recordings, and fully AI-generated videos.
